# Open register

Numerical guards and incomplete oracle work are not passes. Native non-Ok stops below have no finite-attainment or nonexistence proof supplied by the frozen audit rules; the status itself can be correct. Newly certified routes can expose more honest stops without proving existence in the audit oracle.

## Causal attribution gaps

The case reproductions identify the executed fitter and a likely repair for every finding. For families below, this audit has not isolated the exact responsible floating-point operation or acceptance predicate; those nominations remain hypotheses rather than proved causation. Their frozen witnesses are sufficient to test a follow-up repair.

- `beta`: `univariateML-cpp/include/cppunivariate/distributions/beta/mle.hpp:248`; cases `[97, 100]`. Missing: trace the candidate acceptance and public-coordinate error propagation on these held subsets. Likely repair: The observed returned point misses an isolated high-precision stationary reference. Replace step/objective-only acceptance with a relative public-coefficient enclosure (monotone bracket or score/information bound), propagate transformations, and return a numerical failure when the requested width is unresolved. The exact causal arithmetic operation remains open; the executed fitter entry is the source nomination.
